## 11. SEC3 day-two amendment

Implementer: SEC3, 2026-10-19. Commission: day-two fix list 3/4/5/8.
REL5 owns release transitions and compatibility; MIG owns backfills and staging
clock control. This amendment changes security analysis, not runtime authority.

### 11.1 Column read intent

`invariant table.column read only P` protects the value, computations over it,
value-dependent counts/existence/refusals, copied values, assets and delivery.
It does not forbid observing an otherwise public row's existence independently
of the protected value. Binding validates the column and preserves its identity
in every verdict and audit. Effective payload authority is row read AND any
owner-private field gate. Pure projections excluding the field may eliminate
that payload path; predicates, group/order keys, derived values and copied
fields remain observations. Unknown flow stays unproved. A synthetic whole-row
read cannot refute a projection-only column policy: column counterexamples
execute an authored observer in two schema-valid worlds differing only in that
column, with policy false in both. Outputs must differ reproducibly. Maintained
values are recomputed; column-sensitive counts/existence are included. Separate
obligations name unresolved raw-probe, wire, device and opaque flows. A direct
column theorem never silently clears those obligations. Per-column audit
reports effective audience plus operation/derived observers, not only its type.

### 11.2 Reproducible campaigns and reachability

Generated seed ingress contains only authored base rows/fields. Never re-seed
maintained targets or engine-recorded fields. Generated lifetime samples are
live at the campaign clock; principal fields vary independently. Rejected
snapshots are recorded as unsupported, not successful attempts. Existing
fixture histories instead fork the complete SQLite store and asset ledger into
owned isolated memory stores; captures, deadlines, maintained values, lifetimes
and actual media identities survive unchanged. No source store is modified.
Temporary asset directories belong to Store and disappear on drop.

Actors include all principal values in fixtures. Required/optional references
range over fixture rows, not only the first. Short histories visit first steps
breadth first before extending them under a reported work cap. Read and row
assertions are checked after each step. Write/operation assertions judge only
actual committed changes, correct insert/update/delete arms and pre-write
policy state; ambiguous multiple writes, implicit effects or unstable policy
need executed-effect tracing and remain unsupported. A finite trace is never
an induction proof for always or arbitrary histories.

Every counterexample has a machine-readable origin: authored trace from trusted
fixture, trusted-seed-only, or synthetic operation. Only the first establishes
application reachability from the recorded trusted starting state and fails
normal check/test. Seed/synthetic witnesses remain refutations of all-snapshot
claims, visible with --all and JSON, without failing acceptance. Unproved
reachability is never asserted unreachable as a mathematical theorem. Check
loads the same fixture base for reachable histories as test, without HTTP or
business-case execution. A stronger witness must replace a synthetic witness
for the same declaration. --all exposes all witnesses and never changes which
ones fail. No unreachable refutation may print pass/ok beside exit 1.

### 11.3 Actionable authority advice

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies authored caller operations that write role
or group grants trusted by later rules while the effective admission does not
establish independent authority to confer that particular grant. Include
self-signup roles and transitions where a caller can confer a role they lack.
A trusted service seat or an independently authorized administrator may appoint
other roles; holding an unrelated ordinary role does not establish delegation.
Advice names the operation, grant and gate and points to guide staging's
operator first-admin recipe. It is advice, never an implicit runtime refusal
or a proof of malicious intent. Legitimate open enrollment can be intentional.

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE requires an actually mutable/revocable current-role
population and a retained principal/grant branch lacking that role recheck;
cover read AND write branches. Immutable/static role populations do not fire.
N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE excludes public populations and caller-owned role lookups;
only an absent/hidden distinction crossing a narrower private audience warrants
advice. Every security note has a real source location and contributes to the
reported note count. Rule printing preserves connective precedence. Identity
429 responses include a conservative retry delay covering the rate window;
the existing limits and authentication semantics are unchanged.

### 11.4 Evidence

Qualification uses read-only copies of all ten wave-17 high lanes and their
spec-authored invariant sets, plus all 150 earlier schemas. Report caught,
partial and not caught separately; all-snapshot witnesses alone do not count
as a reachable detection. Preserve bad/fixed controls, note counts and source
hashes. The open qualification-gap ticket remains open for unresolved whole
proof/device/opaque obligations. No zero-hole claim follows from finite tests.
use super::*;
impl Parser {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#2-surface — one existing expression grammar.
    pub(super) fn parse_invariant(&mut self) -> Fallible<()> {
        let site = self.take("invariant")?.site;
        let table = self.word()?.value;
        let (target, column) = if self.maybe(".") {
            let mut field = self.word()?.value;
            while self.maybe(".") {field.push('.');field.push_str(&self.word()?.value);}
            if self.maybe("write") { ("write".into(), Some(field)) }
            else { (field, None) }
        } else { (self.take("always")?.value, None) };
        if target != "always" { self.take("only")?; }
        let value = self.expression(0, &[])?;
        self.end_line(&site)?;
        self.document.invariants.push(InvariantDecl { table, target, column, value, site });
        Ok(())
    }

}
//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Obligation {
    pub path: String,
    pub status: Status,
    pub reason: String,
    pub repair: Option<String>,
}
impl Obligation {
    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
        };
        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    }
}

pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
        Ok(super::temporal::admission(table)), policy)];
    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
        let admission = (|| {
            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
            }
            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
            }
            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
        })();
        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
        }
    }
    out
}

fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
        match t {
            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    })
}

//! Short actual operation sequences; never relabel a role snapshot as a history.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — finite history coverage is explicit.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for rule in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{
        fn collect(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(r,out),
            r=>{if let Some(p)=r.service_grant(){out.insert(p.into());}}
        }}collect(rule,&mut out);
    }}
    // Seats can be named only in an operation's require, or only in independent
    // intent. Overapproximate string literals in the compiled artifact; never
    // infer principal reachability or proof from being in this finite cast.
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).expect("schema serialization"),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).expect("program serialization"),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).expect("invariant serialization"),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub(super) fn argument_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fn values(name:&str,kind:&ir::ArgType)->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|v|Value::String(v.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>vec![Value::Id(VIEWER.into()),Value::Id(OTHER.into())],
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{
                let mut cases=vec![Value::Null];cases.extend(values(name,inner)?);cases
            },
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),kind.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,kind) in args {
        let Some(values)=values(name,kind)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history argument needs a typed/asset fixture"));return Vec::new();};
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        for case in &cases{for value in &values{if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("enum/argument Cartesian product exceeds 512; remaining combinations not covered".into());break;}
            let mut c=case.clone();c.insert(name.clone(),value.clone());next.push(c);
        }}cases=next;
    }cases
}
fn pin_refs(args:&mut BTreeMap<String,Value>,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(value)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.last()).and_then(|r|r.field("id").ok()){args.insert(name.clone(),value.clone());}}
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("operation histories need initial fixture rows; schema-only snapshots are tested separately".into());return;}
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000 {out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows; supply a focused fixture".into());return;}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.read_lifetime_sensitive()||t.through.is_some()||t.columns.values().any(C::contains_asset))||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){
        out.unsupported.insert("history replay needs complete temporal/capture/maintenance/asset-ledger fixture support for this schema".into());return;
    }
    let actors=viewers(bound);
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
    for p in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        for args in argument_cases(&p.program.args,out){for viewer in &actors{candidates.push((&p.program,viewer.clone(),args.clone()));}}
    }
    let probes=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read").map(|inv|{
        let mut p=program(&format!("$security.history.read.{}",inv.table),ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"history read".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Variable("$security:row".into())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
        p.args.insert("$security:row".into(),ir::ArgType::Id);p
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut count=0;
    'cases:for first in &candidates{for second in &candidates{
        if count==LIMIT {out.unsupported.insert("short operation sequences capped at 512; unvisited ordered pairs/longer histories not covered".into());break 'cases;}
        count+=1;
        let mut engine=match engine_with(bound,&initial,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break 'cases;}};
        out.sequences+=1;
        let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for (p,viewer,args) in [first,second]{
            let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(_)=>break};let mut args=args.clone();pin_refs(&mut args,p,&before);
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args,result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}){out.refused+=1;}},
                Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;},
            }
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&engine)else{break;};
            for inv in &bound.invariants{
                if inv.target!="always"||out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&!v.history.is_empty()&&v.history.iter().map(|s|&s.operation).eq(steps.iter().map(|s|&s.operation))){continue;}
                for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
                    if before.get(&inv.table).is_some_and(|rs|rs.contains(row)){continue;}
                    if policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row)==Ok(false){
                        out.violations.push(Counterexample{observations:None,history:steps.clone(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"always".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,
                            supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"committed row after actual authored operation sequence".into(),scope:"ordinary operation trace from the recorded schema-valid fixture; finite campaign, not all-history proof".into()});
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        // Observe actual post-history rule admission; do not infer read access
        // from mutation success or from the fixture's role snapshot.
        let Ok(rows)=readback(&engine)else{continue;};
        for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&!v.history.is_empty()&&v.history.iter().map(|s|&s.operation).eq(steps.iter().map(|s|&s.operation))){continue;}for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let Some(key)=row.field("id").ok()else{continue;};
            out.attempted+=1;
            if let Ok(q)=engine.query(&format!("$security.history.read.{}",inv.table),Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::from([("$security:row".into(),key.clone())])).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&q.data!=Value::Null{
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{observations:None,history:steps.clone(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:"$security.history.read".into(),effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:initial.clone(),
                    rule_path:"actual read after two authored operations; current policy false".into(),scope:"ordinary operation trace from the recorded fixture; disconnected-device freshness is separate".into()});
            }
        }}}
    }}
}
//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>serde_json::to_value(&q.data).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
        loop {
            let before=observers.len();
            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
            }}
            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
                    observers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            }
            if observers.len()==before{break;}
        }
        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
        }
        for variant in 0..8 {
            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
            }
            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
            let mut absent=present.clone();absent.remove(&inv.table);
            // Final-exists validation is a pure judgment of this fixed seeded
            // world, before operation/viewer admission. Retain its rejection
            // across observers, never cache execution/resource/policy results.
            let invalid=std::cell::RefCell::new([None::<String>,None]);
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"] {
                for bp in &bound.programs {
                    let p=&bp.program;
                    if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native {continue;}
                    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                    let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                    if writes.iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table||deps.contains(table)||*kind!="update")
                        || !writes.is_empty() && (!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty() || bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.on_delete.is_empty())) {
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired mutation lacks stable policy/implicit-write and deterministic-id reduction",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
                    if !observers.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
                    // Fresh generated ids and opaque viewer/work contexts are
                    // not repeatable functions of these paired inputs.
                    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool {match v{
                        serde_json::Value::String(s)=>s=="NewId"||s=="JobAs",
                        serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"Emit"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"))||m.values().any(opaque),
                        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false,
                    }}
                    if opaque(&json){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque effects excluded from paired determinism",p.name));continue;}
                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.args,variant)else{continue;};
                    let execute=|seeds:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>),String>{
                        let slot=usize::from(std::ptr::eq(seeds,&absent));
                        if let Some(why)=&invalid.borrow()[slot]{return Err(why.clone());}
                        let mut e=match engine(bound,seeds,program("$security.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){
                            Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{
                                if why.ends_with(": trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"){invalid.borrow_mut()[slot]=Some(why.clone());}
                                return Err(why);
                            },
                        };
                        let actual=readback(&e)?;
                        // Judge the hypothetical protected row in each complete
                        // committed world, before any operation can change roles.
                        if policy(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,&row)!=Ok(false){return Err("paired policy is not definitely false in both worlds".into());}
                        observe(&mut e,p,viewer,args.clone()).map(|value|(value,actual))
                    };
                    let ((a,rows),(b,absent_rows))=match (execute(&present),execute(&absent)) {
                        (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                        (Err(e),_)|(_,Err(e))=>{
                            if !e.starts_with("paired policy is not definitely false") {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.name));}
                            continue;
                        },
                    };
                    out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                    if a==b {continue;}
                    // The query order is engine-defined. Recreate both worlds
                    // to decline store-scoped cursors or unstable outputs.
                    if !matches!((execute(&present),execute(&absent)),(Ok((aa,_)),Ok((bb,_))) if aa==a&&bb==b){
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation is not repeatable",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    out.violations.push(Counterexample {history:Vec::new(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),
                        operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows,
                        rule_path:"paired present/absent engine executions differ while the declared privacy policy is false in both".into(),
                        scope:"schema-valid seed-ingress paired worlds; authenticated logical data/refusal only; no application reachability claim".into(),
                        observations:Some(Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows})});
                    break;
                }
                if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
            }
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("paired observations exclude wire sequences/receipts, physical timing and resource/meter differences; HTTP and device histories require separate qualification".into());
}

//! Independent security intent and conservative exposure obligations.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — a direct-rule theorem never clears an unmodeled path.
use super::{catalog::Catalog, Bound};
use crate::{ast, Diagnostic, Site};
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Effect};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub mod kernel;
mod audit;
pub mod challenges;
mod lints;
mod operation;
mod concrete;
mod census;
mod observers;
mod temporal;
mod adoption;
mod images;
pub use audit::{Exposure, Grant};
pub use observers::Obligation;
pub use adoption::{draft, UncoveredExposure};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Invariant {
    pub table: String,
    pub target: String,
    pub column: Option<String>,
    pub predicate: String,
    pub site: Site,
    pub policy: Option<RuleExpr>,
    pub operation: Option<String>,
    pub requirements: Vec<RuleExpr>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub syntax: Option<ast::Expr>,
    pub unsupported: Option<String>,
}
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub invariant: Invariant,
    pub status: Status,
    pub direct_rule: Status,
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub format: u32,
    pub scope: String,
    pub exclusions: Vec<String>,
    pub invariants: Vec<Verdict>,
    pub exposures: Vec<Exposure>,
    pub roles: BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>,
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    pub uncovered: Vec<UncoveredExposure>,
}
impl Report {
    pub fn refuted(&self)->bool {self.invariants.iter().any(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted)}
    pub fn text(&self)->String {
        let mut out=format!("security: {}\n",self.scope);
        for v in &self.invariants {
            out.push_str(&format!("  {:?}: {}.{} {} — direct rule {:?}\n",v.status,v.invariant.table,v.invariant.target,v.invariant.predicate,v.direct_rule));
            for reason in &v.reasons {out.push_str(&format!("    {reason}\n"));}
            for o in &v.obligations {out.push_str(&format!("    {:?}: {} — {}\n",o.status,o.path,o.reason));if let Some(repair)=&o.repair{out.push_str(&format!("      repair: {repair}\n"));}}
            if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample {out.push_str(&format!("    counterexample {}\n",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap()));}
        }
        for note in &self.notes {out.push_str(&format!("  {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));}
        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {
            out.push_str(&format!("role {role} (possible/conditional; row predicates below decide):\n"));
            for entry in entries {out.push_str(&format!("  {entry}\n"));}
        }
        for x in &self.exposures {
            out.push_str(&format!("table {} columns: {}\n",x.table,x.columns.iter().map(|(c,v)|format!("{c} ({v})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}
            for grant in &x.grants {out.push_str(&format!("  inherited grant {}: {}\n",grant.table,grant.predicate)); for w in &grant.writers {out.push_str(&format!("    writer {w}\n"));}}
        }
        out.push_str(&format!("  exclusions: {}\n",self.exclusions.join("; ")));
        out.push_str("exposures without an invariant (conditional role/row coverage; declarations are not proof):\n");
        for x in &self.uncovered {out.push_str(&format!("  {}.{}: {}; rows: {}\n",x.table,x.effect,x.audience,x.row_predicate));}
        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
    }
}
fn effect(word:&str)->Effect {match word {"read"=>Effect::Read,"insert"=>Effect::Insert,"update"=>Effect::Update,_=>Effect::Delete}}
fn effects(inv:&Invariant)->Vec<&str> { if inv.target=="write" {vec!["insert","update"]} else {vec![inv.target.as_str()]} }
/// Analyze declarations without using fixtures as proof assumptions.
pub fn analyze(bound:&Bound)->Report {
    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}
        else if inv.target=="always" {
            // This narrow theorem holds on every validated committed image,
            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.
            let nonnull=match inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap() {
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
                _=>false,
            };
            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
        } else {
            for word in effects(inv) {
                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {temporal::admission(table)}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
                match implication {
                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
                }
                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
                // immutable local fields have the equal-image substitution;
                // owner-field and implicit writers remain separate blockers.
            }
        }
        reasons.extend(blockers(bound,inv));
        let obligations=observers::obligations(bound,inv);
        for o in &obligations {if o.status!=Status::Proved {reasons.push(format!("{}: {}; {}",o.path,o.reason,o.repair.as_deref().unwrap_or("declare an explicit observer policy")));}}
        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let status=if direct==Status::Proved && reasons.is_empty(){Status::Proved}else{Status::Unproved};
        verdicts.push(Verdict{invariant:inv.clone(),status,direct_rule:direct,paths,reasons,counterexample:None,obligations});
    }
    let mut notes=lints::notes(bound,&exposures);
    let missing=adoption::missing(bound);
    if !missing.is_empty() {notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_SECURITY_INTENT",format!("{} tables have access rules and no security invariants: {}; declare the spec's privacy rules as invariants (`guide security`)",missing.len(),missing.join(", ")),"review snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft against the spec; candidates are never auto-accepted").guide("security"));}
    let roles=audit::roles(&exposures);
    let uncovered=adoption::uncovered(bound,&exposures);
    Report{format:1,scope:"logical row/authority intent; all database snapshots; ordinary authenticated viewers including service seats; explicit unproved paths".into(),
        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered}
}
fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
            }
            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
            }
        }
        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
        }
        if inv.target=="read" {
            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
            }
        }
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        for p in &bound.programs {
            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
            }
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));}
//! Closed enum census: a new IR statement/expression/builtin forces review.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — opaque context changes are blockers.
use snapback4_core::ir::{Expr,Stmt,Builtin};
pub(super) fn body(xs:&[Stmt])->bool{xs.iter().all(|s|match s{
    Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Push{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value),
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>expr(condition)&&body(then_body)&&body(else_body),
    Stmt::For{..}=>false,
    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>expr(condition),
    Stmt::Insert{row,..}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{row,..}|Stmt::Upsert{row,..}=>expr(row),
    Stmt::Update{key,patch,..}=>expr(key)&&expr(patch),
    Stmt::WriteTarget{patch,condition,..}=>expr(patch)&&condition.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|body(&c.setup)&&expr(&c.value)),
    Stmt::Delete{key,..}=>expr(key),Stmt::DeleteTarget{..}|Stmt::Break|Stmt::Continue=>true,
    Stmt::Emit{..}=>false,
})}
fn expr(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{
    Expr::Literal(_)|Expr::Variable(_)|Expr::Input(_)=>true,
    Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base),
    Expr::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().all(expr),Expr::Object(xs)=>xs.values().all(expr),
    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>expr(base)&&fields.values().all(expr),
    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>expr(left)&&expr(right),
    Expr::Block{body:xs,value}=>body(xs)&&expr(value),
    Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>r.key.iter().all(expr),
    Expr::Scan(_)|Expr::Merge(_)=>false,
    Expr::Builtin{builtin,args}=>pure(builtin)&&args.iter().all(expr),
}}
fn pure(b:&Builtin)->bool{match b{
    Builtin::JobAs|Builtin::JobQuery{..}=>false,
    Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|Builtin::ReceiptSettled{..}|Builtin::OptionalField{..}|Builtin::Calendar{..}|Builtin::Civil(_)|Builtin::Len|Builtin::Slice|Builtin::Concat|Builtin::Lower|Builtin::Trim|Builtin::Sha256|Builtin::Join|Builtin::Group{..}|Builtin::First|Builtin::Last|Builtin::At|Builtin::Min|Builtin::Max|Builtin::Extremum{..}|Builtin::ExtremumOf{..}|Builtin::Divmod{..}|Builtin::Divide{..}|Builtin::Sort{..}|Builtin::MentionTokens|Builtin::SearchPresent|Builtin::RequireUncapped|Builtin::DeterministicShare|Builtin::Count|Builtin::Sum|Builtin::CountCapped|Builtin::Connected=>true,
}}

//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..} if key.iter().any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&equalities.iter().any(|(_,t)|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))))}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for branch in t.rules.read.disjuncts(){
            if role_test(branch){continue;}
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) && (role_test(&g.rules.insert)||role_test(&g.rules.update)){
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}: `{}` trusts {dep} grants, but this branch does not re-check the reader's current role; existing grants can survive a role change",branch.quarry()),"conjoin the current reader-role probe with the grant branch, or revoke derived grants transactionally on every role change; see guide security");
            }}
            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty() && (role_test(&t.rules.insert)||role_test(&t.rules.update)){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}: stored principal {} remains a reader independently of a later role change",branch.quarry()),"if access requires a current role, conjoin that role in the read branch; historic attribution alone is not current authority");}
        }
    }
    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    let _=exposures;
    notes
}
pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{
        let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
                    });
                }
            }
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
                for f in fields.iter(){
                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
                    }

                }
            });
            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{code,..},..}=>*absent=Some(code.clone()),
                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,&mut None,&mut codes);
            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; callers admitted by the read rule can distinguish these outcomes",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message when existence is private, or tighten the table read rule; the read gate already hides rows it denies");}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
                }}}
                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
                if !clock{continue;}
                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
                    }}
                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
                    });}
                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
                }}
            }
        }
    }
    out
}
//! Conservative permission map; conditional predicates are never universal grants.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — include grant writers and implicit effects.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::ir;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Grant {pub table:String,pub predicate:String,pub writers:Vec<String>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Exposure {pub table:String,pub columns:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub effects:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub operations:Vec<String>,pub paths:Vec<String>,pub grants:Vec<Grant>}
pub(super) fn mentions(json:&serde_json::Value,table:&str)->bool {
    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(k,v)| k=="table"&&v.as_str()==Some(table)||mentions(v,table)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|x|mentions(x,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn authored_refusal(json:&serde_json::Value)->bool {
    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.get("Require").is_some()||fields.values().any(authored_refusal),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(authored_refusal),_=>false}
}
fn writers(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->Vec<String> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
    for effect in [Effect::Insert,Effect::Update] {out.push(format!("{effect} rule: {}",t.rules.for_effect(effect).quarry()));}
    for p in &bound.programs {for (table,kind) in ir::write_tables(&p.program) {if table==name {out.push(format!("{} ({kind}, {}; {})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word(),if p.program.kind==ir::ProgramKind::Job {"system boundary"}else{"caller rules/field authority"}));}}}
    for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {out.push(format!("column {column}: owner .{} can patch under field authority",authority.owner));}
    for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.target==name||m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.table==name){out.push(format!("system maintain {} from {}",m.target,m.source));}}
    if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){out.push(format!("referential actions: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.native)||!bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty()||!bound.schema.effects.is_empty(){out.push("native/worker/external paths: opaque trusted writers (not an absence claim)".into());}
    out.push("operator/system seed, import, restore and activation (trusted boundary)".into());out.sort();out.dedup();out
}
pub(super) fn exposures(bound:&Bound)->Vec<Exposure> {
    bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name,t)| {
        let columns=t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)).map(|(c,k)| {
            let authority=t.written_by.get(c).map(|a|format!("; written by .{}; {}",a.owner,if a.audience{"audience readable"}else{"private to owner"})).unwrap_or_default();
            (c.clone(),format!("{k}{authority}"))
        }).collect();
        let effects=[("read",t.rules.read.quarry()),("insert",t.rules.insert.quarry()),("update",t.update_rule_judged().quarry()),("delete",t.rules.delete.quarry())].into_iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.into(),v)).collect();
        let operations=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name)).map(|p|format!("{} ({})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut paths=vec!["server point/list/filter/view/search/count/exists/live-refetch: ordinary ruled reads".into(),"mutation valued write images: authorized final reread; logical refusals separately unproved".into()];
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some(){paths.push(format!("live rows only: not before {:?}, expire {:?}; server clock gates delivery",t.not_before,t.expire));}
        if let Some(sync)=&t.sync {paths.push(format!("sync/device envelope: {}; device access uses delivered snapshot; leave {:?}",sync.line(),t.leave));}
        if t.columns.values().any(|k|k.contains_asset()){paths.push("asset read: owning-row read rule AND applicable owner-private column authority; upload placement: insert/update authority".into());}
        if let Some(through)=&t.through {paths.push(format!("read through {}.{} -> {} (capture/live reduction unproved)",name,through.reference,through.parent));}
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.source==*name||m.target==*name {paths.push(format!("derived {} -> {} (independent aggregate audience)",m.source,if m.target.starts_with("$column:"){ "maintained column" }else{&m.target}));}}
        if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){paths.push(format!("incoming referential actions bypass child rules: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
        let mut grants=Vec::new();let mut pending=Vec::new();let mut visited=BTreeSet::new();
        for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete] {for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((dep,r.quarry()));}}
        while let Some((dep,predicate))=pending.pop(){if !visited.insert(dep.clone()){continue;}let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep)else{continue};
            grants.push(Grant{table:dep.clone(),predicate,writers:writers(bound,&dep)});
            for r in [&g.rules.read,&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update,&g.rules.delete]{for next in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((next,r.quarry()));}}
        }
        Exposure{table:name.clone(),columns,effects,operations,paths,grants}
    }).collect()
}
/// Role labels retain complete predicates, including relationships. Every
/// entry is conditional; a roster's existence is never assumed for all users.
pub(super) fn roles(exposures:&[Exposure])->BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>> {
    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for x in exposures {for (effect,predicate) in &x.effects {
        if predicate=="deny"{continue;}
        let label=if predicate=="allow"||predicate=="public"||predicate.starts_with("public "){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")};
        out.entry(label).or_default().push(format!("{} {effect}: {predicate}; operations: {}",x.table,x.operations.join(", ")));
    }}out
}

//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];
mod paired;
mod timed;
